  423 | 		let compared:number = 0;
  424 | 		let skipped:number = 0;
  425 | 		let mismatched:number = 0;
  426 | 		for (const product of results) {
  427 | 			const filter:string = genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("filter"));
  428 | 			const title:string = genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("title"));
  429 | 			const keyFeatures:string = genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("keyFeatures"));
  430 | 			const about :string= genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("about"));
  431 | 			const techDetails:string = genericUtility.safeLower(product.get("techDetails"));
  432 | 			const match = rangeMatch(filter, ["screen size"], { title, keyFeatures, about, techDetails });
  433 | 
  434 | 			// 4. Comparable: without a Screen Size row in the spec table nothing can be said, so the product is skipped.
  435 | 			if (match.status === "unknown") {
  436 | 				console.warn(`[${testName}] No usable Screen Size row for filter '${filter}' on product '${title}'; not counting it as a mismatch`);
  437 | 				skipped++;
  438 | 				continue;
  439 | 			}
  440 | 			compared++;
  441 | 
  442 | 			if (match.status === "mismatch") {
  443 | 				mismatched++;
  444 | 				console.error(`[${testName}] Mismatch found for filter '${filter}' on product '${title}' (row says: ${match.rowValue})`);
  445 | 				console.debug(`[${testName}] Key Features: ${keyFeatures}`);
  446 | 				console.debug(`[${testName}] About: ${about}`);
  447 | 				console.debug(`[${testName}] Tech Details: ${techDetails}`);
  448 |
